The row law is imported from queue_floor (is_done_row, QUEUE_PATHS) and the suite proves the import by patching is_done_row, so a row is still decided in exactly one place. Stamp forms: [<marker> YYYY-MM-DD R<n>] and [<marker> R<n>] resolve, where the marker family is the words this house actually writes on a closed row (done, claimed+done, claimed, negative, closed); a date alone is DATE-ONLY debt because several rounds share a day; an empty or shapeless bracket is BAD-STAMP; no bracket is UNSTAMPED. The row own seed round is the first R<n> after the stamp is removed, so a stamp EARLIER than that round is STAMP-BEFORE-SEED with both rounds named, while a row naming no round is never accused of a violation it cannot have committed. 0、四道率与 smoothed 全 None、disclaimer 常驻。**AC-RFP2 分母律**=分母是**全部购买**（含已退款成交；退款在 UNIQUE(purchase_id) 下一笔购买最多一次退款，故 refund_count ≤ purchase_count 且 rate∈[0,1]）；non_refunded==purchase−refund、net_spent==gross−refunded、rate==refund/purchase（0 购买则 None，不除零不造假零）。**AC-RFP3 金额轴独立**=refund_amount_rate==refunded/gross（gross 0 则 None）是与计数轴**不同的量**（夹具中 bob 计数率 0.25 而金额率 3/26≈0.1154 实证两者分离）。**AC-RFP4 市场基准**=market_* 取全市场计数、market_refund_rate==market_refund_count/market_purchase_count（0 则 None）、refund_rate_delta==rate−market_rate（任一侧 None 则 None）；空市场全 None。**AC-RFP5 低样本归一**=smoothed_refund_rate==(refund_count+K*market_rate)/(purchase_count+K)，K=smoothing_strength=10，purchase 0 或 market None 则 None；**收缩恒等式**=|smoothed−market|==|rate−market|*n/(n+K)（容差 1e-12）；夹具（市场率 10/45≈0.2222）下买 1 退 1 的 raw 率 1.0 高于买 20 退 8 的 0.4，而 smoothed 0.2929 **低于** 0.3407——低样本不因样本小反而显得更危险，这正是种子要求的归一化判据。**AC-RFP6 退款权威与生命周期**=退款只经 append-only apimarket_refunds 派生，一笔新退款一步内同时改动本面与 api_buyer_profile 的对应值（计数与金额四项跨面相等）；退役/relist 对本面逐字节无影响；他买家/卖家侧零渗透。**AC-RFP7 门族与纯读**=七式坏账户形（None/""/"  "/123/[]/{...}/裸名）全 - [x] P3 explore 自研新面孔（R2111）apimarket **退款登记簿**：市场级退款面（api_refund_register：窗口+卖方+挂牌三重过滤·按月/按卖方/按行三段对账·金额守恒）+ 报表 Refund register 段 + REPORT_SCHEMA 5→6 加法。缺口实证=退款只能按 purchase_id（api_refund）或按买家（buyer history）取，没有任何面孔回答「本月全市场退了多少、哪家卖方退款最多、异常在哪」，运营对账必须手工并表。研究 AC-AMRR1..AC-AMRR8 预注册于代码之前。AC-AMRR 前缀 / api_refund_register / REFUND_KEYS / refund_block / test_apimarket_report_refundregister.py / ledger-apimarket-refundregister 全串 git grep -F 零占用 rc=1（2026-10-25）。【**AC-AMRR1 契约与派生集**】=report['refunds'] 固定十三键 {kind,since_utc,until_utc,dev_account,listing_id,refund_count,amount,dev_share,platform_share,months,by_dev,refunds,disclaimer}；refund_count==len(refunds)；零退款世界诚实空（count 0 且三段均为空表）。【**AC-AMRR2 同源无缓存**】=报表值==直调 api_refund_register 同参值；采集后新增一笔退款下次采集即现； dev_account/listing_id 过滤精确收窄（他卖方/他挂牌零行）。【**AC-AMRR3 半开窗口**】=refunded_utc 恰等 since 入、恰等 until 出；since>until 报 E_AM_BAD_WINDOW；窗口与卖方过滤可叠加且结果==两次过滤交集。【**AC-AMRR4 金额守恒**】=每行 dev_share+platform_share==amount；months 合计==by_dev 合计==总额；每行金额与 apimarket_refunds 存储值逐字段相等。【**AC-AMRR5 加法契约**】=REPORT_SCHEMA 5→6；JSON 仍恰 schema_version/report/attention 三键；market/board/sellers/buyers（months,profile,todo,watch_alerts,history）/activity/dossiers/attention 各块值不变。【**AC-AMRR6 门族 fail-closed**】=坏窗口/坏过滤十一式全拒（E_AM_BAD_WINDOW/E_AM_BAD_ARGS），拒绝前后十三表摘要与 ledger_tx 计数零变化。【**AC-AMRR7 纯读与确定性**】=一次 collect 十三表摘要与 ledger_tx 计数不变；两同种子世界 HTML 与 JSON 逐字节同一。【**AC-AMRR8 HTML 面与降级回归**】=页面含 Refund register 段三段表头与诚实空态；恶意 reason 转义；缺键旧报表字典仍渲染；六个既有报表套件全绿；runner 标签已注册。[done 2026-10-25 R2111]（前窗中断遗留的产物由 R2111 验证收口：套件双跑 rc=0/0 判据行同一 sha16 189ab027aac404a4=qa/apimarket-refundregister-R2111.log+-2·电池 RUNNER PASS 316/316 suites+controls 7/7 564.3s=qa/reconcile-all-R2111.log·矩阵按 R2111 证据重生成 316/2636 后 --check byte-identical·census DRIFT-CLEAN files=1771 flags=0·呈看件 preview/apimarket-report-refunds.html+json schema 6）

> 认领制：自正典任务板 `tasks.md` 迁六件起版（CPH4 Labs 巡检批 2026-09-24 代迁）。取最上一条未完成项为当轮焦点；完成标 `[done 日期]` 并同步勾选 `tasks.md` 对应项；做不完拆细、剩余部分写回顶行；全部依赖 CEO 物理件的项=不可认领（如实待物理件，不造活）。
> 双板律：本板=认领/进度工作面；`tasks.md`=正典任务板。两板同六件起版，完成态两板同步。
